## 0. Completeness

Expected 5 seeds for every dataset x detector x experiment x policy. 390/390 cells complete. Superseded duplicate rows: 0 (listed in `duplicates.csv`).

Dataset flags carried into every table: **psm**: anomalies not rare (34% of test windows); **exathlon**: single trace, 5 informative features; **ghl**: simulated plant; **threew**: 35% of cells imputed; faults cut 30 min after onset.

## 2. Threshold policies: coverage, calibration, deployable F1 (Q1-Q3)

exp_6, deployed settings (lambda_temp = 0.0, ACI gamma = 0.005). Coverage = fraction of true-normal test windows kept normal; gap = coverage - (1 - alpha) (negative = under-coverage). F1/P/R here are at the **conformal threshold** (deployable).

Pre-stated criteria (ANALYSIS_PLAN "what would count against"): Q1 fails if sliding is within 3 pts on most datasets; Q2 fails if ACI misses by > 3 pts on more than a few datasets. ## 8. Sensitivity to the dataset set (EXPLORATORY, added after results)

Not part of the pre-registered plan; added on 2026-10-21 after the results were seen, to check whether any conclusion rests on a single dataset (ANALYSIS_PLAN.md, Deviations). Each row repeats the main tests with the named dataset(s) removed: leave-one-dataset-out for every dataset, plus SMAP and Daphnet together. It is a robustness check, not a replacement for the main analysis, which uses all 10 datasets. Columns give the largest Holm-adjusted p over the 12 detector x alpha tests (coverage, recall) or the 3 detectors (GA-CE validity), and the median ROC-AUC difference to IsolationForest. With n = 10 the smallest attainable Wilcoxon p is 0.002 (0.023 after Holm over 12), with n = 9 it is 0.004 (0.047), with n = 8 it is 0.008 (0.094), so removing datasets limits attainable significance mechanically.
